A -> Commit and branch are related but not interchangeable. A commit records a snapshot of tracked content in a version-control repository with metadata and a message. Learn the definition and constraints of each before deciding which applies.

Q -> A search algorithm halves a sorted search range each step. What time complexity is typical?
